### 2026-10-20 11:52:27  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

US 10-Year Yield Holds Gains — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note held around 5.13% on Tuesday after climbing in the previous session, as investors assessed the Federal Reserve’s policy outlook ahead of its October 27–28 meeting. Market participants are also awaiting the latest Personal Consumption Expenditures price index, the Fed’s preferred inflation gauge. Markets currently price in an 83% probability that the central bank will leave interest rates unchanged next week, while assigning a 94% chance of a rate hike in December. On Friday, Fed Chair Kevin Warsh refrained from signaling the likely outcome of this month’s FOMC meeting but stated that stronger-than-expected inflation had not changed his assessment of the US economy. Meanwhile, persistently high oil prices continued to fuel inflation concerns, helping sustain upward pressure on bond yields worldwide.

### 2026-10-20 11:20:22  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Corn Falls to 9-Week Low — Corn futures fell to around $4.6 per bushel, reaching their lowest level in nine-weeks as favorable harvest weather and expectations of a record-large US crop weighed on prices. Scattered rains last week stalled fieldwork in parts of the Midwest, but drier weather forecasted through the end of the week is expected to accelerate harvesting, bringing more supply to market. US farmers now harvested 44% of the corn crop, below trade expectations, according to the USDA’s weekly crop progress report. In its October outlook, the USDA also raised its 2026/27 US corn yield estimate to 181.2 bushels per acre from 178.5 and projected production at 16.03 billion bushels, while ending stocks were forecast at 1.849 billion bushels. Export demand added pressure on prices, as US corn export inspections fell to 26.47 million bushels in the week ended October 15, well below analysts’ expectations of 43.3 million bushels and the lowest weekly total since mid-October 2024.

### 2026-10-20 11:19:39  #宏觀

【國家發展改革委：兩批88個投資專案吸引民企300餘次諮詢，部分專案取得實質性進展】10月20日，國家發展改革委民營經濟發展局副局長劉丹在發改委10月份新聞釋出會上回答記者提問。劉丹表示，9月份國家發展改革委分兩批向民營企業公開推介88個投資專案，計劃引入民間資本311億元。這些優質專案，是向廣大民營企業發出的一份“誠摯邀約”，給專案方與民營企業架起了一座橋樑，希望以專案推介為抓手，著力破解專案投資資訊供需不對稱難題，持續拓寬民間投資渠道，切實保障民營企業既有“入場券”、又有“專案單”。整體來看，兩批專案推介有三個方面特點：一是精準貼合企業投資需求。在與民營企業常態化溝通交流中，不少企業向我們反映在交通、物流、水利、能源、先進製造業等領域有較強的投資意願，我們堅持分批次、小規模的原則，篩選了一批質量高、收益穩、預期好的投資專案向民營企業推介，讓民營企業能夠看得見、投得進、有回報。二是線上線下拓展推介渠道。一方面組織民營企業、行業協會召開專題對接座談會；另一方面依託我委微信公眾號、官方網站公開發布專案清單，不斷提升專案覆蓋面和知曉度。三是參與方式更加靈活多樣。企業可根據自身經營實際，透過參股、控股、特許經營等多種方式參與專案建設，不設定不合理門檻。劉丹表示，專案公開推介後，各方反應積極。據初步統計，民營企業與專案單位開展對接、諮詢累計300餘次，部分熱點投資專案收到20餘家民營企業的合作意向，一些專案已經取得實質性進展：比如，貴州固態電池生產基地專案吸引湖南某民營企業簽約投資5000萬元，四川鐵路物流園區專案吸引北京某民營企業意向投資17億元等等。劉丹表示，國家發展改革委將對公開推介的專案實施清單化管理，定期排程進展，及時協調解決堵點難點問題，全力推動專案清單儘快轉化為實實在在的落地投資。（證券時報）

【NDRC to routinely pitch projects to private firms, boost support and tackle arrears】NDRC deputy director Liu Dan said the commission will routinely promote investment projects to private enterprises while strengthening top‑level design and deploying a “policy+project+scenario” coordinated approach to remove implicit barriers and restore investor confidence. The commission will step up efforts to resolve arrears owed to private firms and orderly open major scientific research infrastructure to private access.

### 2026-10-20 11:15:17  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy

【Asian spot LNG jumps to highest since 2022】Asian spot LNG jumped to $30.4/MMBtu on Monday, the strongest level since December 2022, traders said. The surge followed reports a tugboat near Khor Fakkan, UAE, was struck by two objects and a UKMTO alert that a small fast boat fired on an LNG carrier near Yemen last Saturday. Attacks on oil and gas shipping in the Red Sea and Strait of Hormuz have increased. US–Iran tensions have effectively trapped most of Qatar’s LNG output — previously about 20% of global supply — inside the Strait of Hormuz, reducing contracted availability and forcing buyers toward higher-priced spot cargoes. With northern-hemisphere winter demand rising, spot prices are roughly three times levels before the late-February escalation.

【教育部等三部門部署防控兒童青少年高度近視】近日，教育部、國家衛生健康委、國家疾控局聯合印發《關於防控兒童青少年高度近視的通知》，明確了高度近視的內涵和危害，高度近視通常是指近視度數大於等於600度或眼軸長度大於等於26毫米的屈光不正狀態。兒童青少年近視程度一旦發展為高度近視，可能會演變成病理性近視，增加發生視網膜脫離、黃斑病變、青光眼、白內障等併發症風險，造成不可逆的視力損害，是致盲性眼病的主要原因之一。《通知》就主動預防高度近視發生提出了6個方面的防控舉措。包括指導中小學校落實教室、宿舍、增加戶外活動。中小學校要嚴格落實中小學生每天綜合體育活動時間不低於2小時，推行“課間15分鐘”。引導已近視的學生進一步增加戶外活動時間。加強日常護眼。中小學生可透過經常性遠眺等方式放鬆眼睛，保持“一拳一尺一寸”的正確讀寫姿勢，堅持規範做眼保健操。

As of press time, foreign investors had net sold 636.7 bln won in the Korean securities market. Institutional investors net sold 467.0 bln won, with financial investment firms net selling 241.8 bln won and pension funds 159.0 bln won. Singapore Stocks Trade at Over 3-Month Low — The Straits Times Index (STI) fell 25 points, or 0.5%, to 5,387 in Tuesday morning trade, extending losses from the previous session, mainly weighed down by the tech, finance, non-energy minerals, and producer manufacturing sectors. The broader index declined to its lowest level since July 8, amid caution ahead of the release of September inflation data due this week, with the market expecting inflation to remain steady at 2.3%, its highest level since July 2024. 【金融監管總局副局長叢林：高度警惕長期低利率環境下壽險利差損風險】金融監管總局副局長叢林10月20日在2026金融街論壇年會保險大會上表示，統籌發展與安全，牢牢守住不發生系統性風險的底線。她提到，要高度警惕長期低利率環境下壽險利差損風險，最佳化負債結構與成本，有序消化高成本的存量保單，提升跨週期經營能力。首先，要健全全鏈條的風險防控體系。她表示，保險機構要夯實自身的內控合規防線，完善制度流程，層層壓實責任，建立起常態化的風險監測預警機制；最佳化監測指標，實現風險的早識別、早預警和早暴露，完善各類風險應急處置預案，做到風險發現在早，處置在小。其次，緊盯重點領域的風險防控。她提到，要高度警惕長期低利率環境下壽險利差損風險，最佳化負債結構與成本，有序消化高成本的存量保單，提升跨週期經營能力；強化資產負債聯動管理，健全利率傳導與負債成本調節機制，築牢穩健經營的基礎；嚴密監測資產端的風險，最佳化資產配置結構，強化流動性風險防控，守護保險賠付生命線；還要密切地跟蹤新業態催生的各類新型風險，加強聲譽風險管理。最後，有序穩妥化解中小金融機構風險。“要按照市場化法治化原則，有序推動經營偏弱、定位模糊、風險突出的保險機構實行分類風險處置、結構最佳化與業務轉型，透過綜合施策來穩妥化解存量風險，有效遏制新增風險。”她說。（上證報）

China 10Y Yield Hovers Near 2025 Low — China’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 1.65% on Tuesday, hovering near its lowest level since June 2025 after the People’s Bank of China left its benchmark lending rates unchanged. The central bank kept the one-year loan prime rate at 3% and the five-year rate at 3.5%, holding both at record lows for a 17th consecutive month. The decision followed data showing China’s economy grew 4.5% year-over-year in Q3 2026, accelerating from a three-year low in Q2 and beating market forecasts. The rebound keeps Beijing’s full-year growth target of 4.5%–5.0% within reach, giving policymakers more room to delay broad-based monetary easing after introducing fresh targeted support measures. Meanwhile, PBoC Governor Pan Gongsheng said the central bank would place greater emphasis on short-term interest rates, with the overnight interbank repo rate potentially serving as a benchmark for bonds and financial derivatives while the LPR remains the benchmark for corporate and personal loans.

Offshore Yuan Extends Gains to Over 4-Year High — The offshore yuan strengthened to around 6.68 per dollar on Tuesday, extending its winning streak to a fourth straight session and hitting its strongest level since June 2022, as stronger economic growth reduced the urgency for further monetary easing. The PBoC kept the one-year LPR unchanged at 3% and the five-year rate at 3.5%, holding both at record lows for the 17th consecutive meeting. China’s GDP grew 4.5% yoy in Q3 2026 from Q2’s three-year low and keeping the government’s FY growth target of 4.5%–5% within reach. This gives policymakers more room to hold off on additional easing after rolling out fresh targeted measures to support the domestic economy. Meanwhile, PBoC Governor Pan said the PBoC would continue reforming its monetary policy framework, with greater emphasis on short-term interest rates. The LPR could remain the benchmark for small business and personal loans, while the overnight interbank repo rate could serve as a benchmark for bonds and financial derivatives.

【我國發布首批136項標誌性重大應用場景專案】記者10月20日從國家發展改革委新聞釋出會上獲悉，國家發展改革委會同有關部門推出首批136項標誌性重大應用場景專案，為場景培育和開放提供重要牽引。國家發展改革委政策研究室副主任、新聞發言人李超在釋出會上表示，具體看，這批專案要完成的任務可以用三個關鍵詞來概括。一是驗證，推動新技術新產品新業態加快成熟、轉化應用。比如，智慧礦山領域相關場景，將啟用超過2800座礦山的場景資源，為雲端計算、工業物聯網等提供驗證空間，加快轉化為高效、安全的礦山生產新方式。二是開放，支援各類經營主體共享發展機遇。比如，腦機介面領域相關驗證場景，將圍繞腦機診斷、治療、輔助、消費等環節，建設腦機介面資料平臺、中試平臺、場景應用中心等服務底座，為相關企業開展產品驗證、拓展市場空間提供支撐。三是示範，為更大範圍應用提供樣板。比如，電力能源領域的冀北億千瓦級新能源服務京津冀能源綠色轉型和產業協同發展場景，以及新型儲能電力系統應用場景等，將圍繞高比例新能源消納、新型儲能體系建設、綠色產業協同發展等場景需求，佈局構網型柔性直流輸電、柔性負荷調控、風光儲主動支撐與彙集組網等系統性工程，為能源體系綠色低碳轉型提供樣板。（新華社）

【UBS cuts Zijin Gold International PT 3.4% to HKD196, retains Buy】UBS cut Zijin Gold International (02259.HK) PT by 3.4% to HKD196 and kept a Buy rating. Shenwan Hongyuan chief economist Zhao Wei said the domestic economy will undergo an N-shaped recovery, with Q4 likely characterised by sustained strong exports and marginal domestic-demand repair. September data showed marginal improvement in both external and domestic demand; overall growth is expected to drift modestly higher in Q4. Exports: short-term China‑EU trade disruption is limited; an AI-led capex cycle and a rebound in US imports should support continued high export growth. Investment: developer financing stress will likely continue to weigh on property investment, but faster implementation of existing domestic policy and earlier profit gains should provide lagged support, leaving overall investment on a recovery path in Q4. Consumption: household spending should continue a modest rebound and remain broadly stable.

Zinc Holds Rebound — Zinc futures hovered above $3,700 per ton, holding onto a rebound from a more than two-month low of $3,664.9 per ton touched on October 16, amid persistent near-term supply tightness. Tight supply is particularly evident in Western markets, where zinc production fell 3.4% year-on-year in the first half of 2026 amid high energy costs and limited raw material availability. Investors are also watching the outcome of Nyrstar’s strategic review of its loss-making Budel smelter in the Netherlands, with potential operational changes posing a threat to supply. Meanwhile, China’s refined zinc production fell 1.9% month-on-month and 6.4% year-on-year in September. Adding to supply concerns, inventories monitored by the Shanghai Futures Exchange declined 1.5% from the previous week, providing further support for prices.

【Indonesia moves to head off possible MSCI downgrade; stocks rebound】Indonesia's stock market rebounded after authorities unveiled measures aimed at averting a potential two-notch downgrade by MSCI. The Jakarta Composite Index rose as much as 2.7%, recouping part of an about 15% decline over the prior two sessions — the steepest sell-off since 1989 and leaving Indonesia the world's worst-performing market. The Indonesia Stock Exchange said after a Monday meeting with MSCI representatives it will exclude companies with highly concentrated ownership from its benchmark sample and expand ownership disclosure, requiring clarity on whether investors holding 1% or more are related parties. MSCI has said it is considering downgrading Indonesia from an emerging to a standalone market — a step more severe than January's warning to possibly reclassify it as a frontier market — citing opaque ownership structures, suspected coordinated trading and insufficient progress on this year's regulatory reforms.

Australia 10Y Yield Trades Below 5.3% — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield traded below 5.3%, struggling to recover from a recent five-week low as investors awaited further clues on the policy outlook. Focus is on RBA Deputy Governor Andrew Hauser, who is due to speak this week and could provide insight into the prospects for a fifth rate hike this year. An RBA official recently noted that the labor market may be cooling faster than anticipated, though the next inflation report on October 28 remains key to the policy outlook. Core inflation in the third quarter is expected to rise above the RBA’s 2–3% target and its 3.3% forecast. Governor Michele Bullock and other officials will face lawmakers that day, offering an opportunity to address the latest figures. Markets now price in roughly a one-in-three chance of a November hike, rising to 56% for December. Meanwhile, elevated oil prices amid ongoing hostilities in the Middle East and Eastern Europe, heightened inflation concerns and pushed global yields higher.

Hong Kong Stocks Fall as AI Concerns Persist — The Hang Seng Index fell around 1.0%, or 223 points, to about 23,960 on Tuesday as Hong Kong equities resumed trading after Monday’s public holiday, with technology services and retail shares leading losses. Technology stocks remained under pressure amid concerns over the financing of AI infrastructure, elevated borrowing costs and uncertainty over whether heavy investment would deliver sufficient returns. Notable laggards were Lenovo (-5.8%), Tencent fell (-2.0%), Innovent Biologics(-4.4%), MiniMax (-1.4%), and Semiconductor Manufacturing International Corporation (-1.5%). China's decision to keep its benchmark lending rates unchanged at 3.00% and 3.50% offered no additional monetary easing, although stronger-than-expected third-quarter economic growth reduced the immediate pressure for stimulus. Retail trade fell 2.01% and producer manufacturing declined 1.38%, while gains in energy minerals (+0.16%) and electronic technology (+0.34%) provided limited support.

### 2026-10-20 09:58:32  #國際

【日本發射火星衛星探測器】日本火星衛星探測器20日凌晨發射升空。該探測器將對火星的衛星“火衛一”進行詳細觀測，並取樣返回地球。 日本宇宙航空研究開發機構的直播畫面顯示，當地時間20日4時41分（北京時間3時41分），該探測器搭乘H3火箭10號從日本南部鹿兒島縣的種子島宇宙中心升空。發射約1小時11分鐘後，火箭將探測器送入火星轉移軌道並和探測器分離。 火星衛星探測專案由日本宇宙航空研究開發機構主導，美國國家航空航天局、法國國家航天研究中心、德國航空航天中心和歐洲航天局等參與。 探測器將用約一年時間飛抵火星附近，進入環繞火星的“準衛星軌道”。在環繞火星執行期間，探測器將對“火衛一”進行詳細觀測，調查“火衛一”上幾十個地點的地形、化學組成等，並確定在“火衛一”上著陸的地點。此後，探測器將嘗試在“火衛一”著陸並採集10克以上的衛星表面砂子和岩石樣本。按計劃，探測器將於2030年開始返程，並於2031年帶著樣本回到地球。 “火衛一”距離火星約6000公里，是一個土豆樣的不規則天體，其直徑只有月球的約160分之一，表面佈滿大大小小的隕石坑。關於火星衛星的起源，比較主流的有“大撞擊說”和“捕獲說”兩種假說。解開“火衛一”的起源之謎將幫助人們理解太陽系的形成及太陽系內的物質輸送。(新華社)

Silver Remains Under Pressure — Silver prices fell to around $56.5 per ounce on Tuesday, extending recent losses as elevated oil prices heightened inflation concerns and reinforced expectations of tighter monetary policy. The metal also faced pressure from a stronger US dollar and rising bond yields. Oil prices remained firm amid ongoing hostilities in the Middle East and Eastern Europe, while Iran continued to assert its control over the Strait of Hormuz. Meanwhile, the US Federal Reserve is widely expected to keep interest rates unchanged next week, with markets pricing in roughly a 94% probability of a rate hike in December. Elsewhere, the European Central Bank is expected to implement at least one additional 25-basis-point rate increase this year to address persistent inflationary pressures. Bank of Japan Governor Kazuo Ueda also noted that underlying inflation was “quite close” to the central bank’s 2% target, signaling the possibility of further monetary tightening.

South Korean Shares Plunge on AI Concerns — The benchmark KOSPI plunged more than 3% to around 5,595 on Tuesday, reversing gains from the previous session, as renewed selling in semiconductor stocks reflected persistent concerns over the sustainability of AI investment. Samsung Electronics and SK hynix slid more than 3% and 6%, respectively, as worries over debt-funded AI infrastructure and elevated borrowing costs continued to cloud the sector's outlook. Other notable declines included SK Square (-7.1%), LG Energy Solution (-3.9%), Hyundai Motor (-4.8%), Hanwha Aerospace (-11.3%), Doosan Enerbility (-6.6%), HD Hyundai Heavy Industries (-7.4%), and Kia Corporation (-3.5%). Meanwhile, South Korea's planned 800 trillion won investment in four semiconductor fabs and SK hynix's 2 trillion won supplier financing program aim to expand domestic chipmaking capacity and strengthen the semiconductor supply chain, although infrastructure and US trade-related hurdles remain.

If the Republican Party wins control of the House and Senate, I will send $5,000 to every adult citizen of the United States." You must vote Republican." ### 2026-10-20 09:28:34  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Australian Dollar Holds Firm — The Australian dollar held its recent gains near $0.70, remaining above multi-month lows as investors awaited further clues on the policy outlook. RBA Deputy Governor Andrew Hauser is due to speak on Wednesday, which could provide insight into the prospects for a fifth rate hike this year. An RBA official recently noted that the labor market may be cooling faster than anticipated, though the next inflation report on October 28 remains key to the policy outlook. Core inflation is expected to rise by 0.9–1.0% in the third quarter, potentially lifting the annual rate to 3.5–3.6%, above the RBA’s 2–3% target and its 3.3% forecast. Governor Michele Bullock and other officials will also face lawmakers that day, offering an opportunity to address the latest figures. Markets now price in roughly a one-in-three chance of a November hike, rising to 56% for December. Elsewhere, the Aussie also drew support from a broadly weaker euro as political concerns in France weighed on the single currency.

【青花郎四連陽再創月新高 青花汾20重返400元 五糧液普五回落5元仍守住800大關】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，10月20日中國白酒業12大單品的終端零售均價四漲四跌四平，漲跌品種的數量持平，累計漲跌幅度也相抵，整體價盤在連續上行後轉入平盤整理。上漲端，精品茅臺上漲3元至2466元，二連陽繼續走高，價格重新回到近期高位；青花汾20上漲3元至400元，連續第二日回升，收復400元整數關口；古井貢古20上漲1元至541元，四連陽累計大漲18元，延續近期強勢修復行情；青花郎上漲4元至714元，四連漲持續走強，進一步重新整理近一個月價格新高。下跌端，飛天茅臺下跌2元至1791元，逼近1790元一線；五糧液普五八代下跌5元至802元，連續兩日攀升後明顯回落，但依然守在800元關口之上；五糧液1618下跌1元至830元，連續三日平盤的均衡打破；國窖1573下跌3元至894元，昨日創下近一個月新高後回撥，900元關口前漲勢暫歇。持平方面，茅臺1935維持684元不變，昨日小幅下跌後轉入橫盤；洋河夢之藍M6+維持611元，連續兩日下跌後暫時企穩；習酒君品保持640元不變，結束此前連續回撥；水晶劍南春維持411元，昨日回落後進入平盤整理。今日12大單品的終端零售總價結束了此前連續四日的上漲，漲跌兩端力量基本均衡，青花郎、精品茅臺等品種繼續走強，而五糧液普五八代、國窖1573等出現不同程度回撥。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10784元，與昨日持平，仍維持在近13天以來的高位水平。

【高市早苗重申：不會發行赤字國債為食品銷售稅減稅提供資金】日本首相高市早苗重申，不會依靠專項赤字填補國債，為食品消費稅減稅籌措資金。此前她的發言引發市場猜測，認為其立場出現轉變。高市早苗在 X 平臺發文稱，她並未排除為整體國家預算發行國債，但不會藉助專項赤字填補國債，來彌補臨時性消費稅減稅造成的稅收缺口。她寫道，政府這一立場 “保持不變”。週一早些時候她在國會發言，部分媒體報道出現誤讀，因此她出面澄清自身立場。在參議院預算委員會會議上，被問及減稅資金來源時，高市早苗表示，不排除發行國債。高市早苗稱：“關於食品飲料消費稅下調的資金來源，我們將在預算編制改革的大框架內，審慎評估財政規模，在監控稅收走勢、同時對收支兩端開展審查的前提下，實現債務率下降。”她表示：“如同上一財年，我們將透過初始預算與補充預算，合理管控年度國債總髮行規模。這並不代表我們完全不發行國債。”市場高度關注高市早苗如何在不進一步加重國家債務負擔的前提下落實減稅政策。該減稅方案預計每年造成 5 萬億日元（摺合 315 億美元）稅收損失。這項臨時減稅政策計劃於 4 月啟動，為期兩年。

【民航局與上海市政府座談】10月19日，民航局與上海市政府在上海舉行座談會，共商上海國際航空樞紐建設工作。民航局黨組書記、局長宋志勇，上海市委副書記、市長朱忠明出席並講話。宋志勇說，民航局認真貫徹落實習近平總書記對民航工作重要指示批示精神，全力推動民航高水平安全與高質量發展。感謝上海市對民航事業各項工作給予大力支援。今年以來，上海機場客貨吞吐規模持續增長，航空樞紐門戶功能不斷增強。民航局積極支援上海“五個中心”建設，制發重點工作任務清單，細化落實有關支援舉措，積極推進浦東機場四期擴建等工程建設，持續推動最佳化長三角空域結構、完善上海航空樞紐航線網路等工作。下一步，民航局將按照《民用航空發展“十五五”規劃》，一如既往做好各項工作，支援上海最佳化客貨航班時刻、空域結構，提升國際中轉功能，推動上海高質量建設國際航空樞紐。朱忠明說，高質量建設上海國際航空樞紐，是提升上海國際航運中心能級、加快“五個中心”建設的重要保障，是推進長三角一體化發展、落實區域協調發展戰略的重要舉措，是提升民航國際競爭力、服務交通強國建設的重要支撐。上海將錨定全面建成具有世界影響力的全方位門戶複合型國際航空樞紐這一戰略目標，聚力夯實硬體支撐，提升國際航空樞紐能級，推進數字化、智慧化、綠色化轉型，守牢航空安全底線，推動上海國際航空樞紐建設取得新進展、邁上新臺階。希望進一步加強局市合作，共同推動重大改革、重大專案、重大政策落地，為譜寫交通強國建設的民航新篇章作出更大貢獻。

### 2026-10-20 09:06:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Interest Rate #Importance 2

China Holds LPR Rates at Record Lows for 17th Month — The People's Bank of China kept its key lending rates at record lows for the 17th straight month in October 2026, even as the yuan continued to strengthen amid strong economic data that met expectations. The decision reflected caution about the impact of the Middle East conflict, despite Q3 GDP growth exceeding expectations. The one-year loan prime rate (LPR), the benchmark for most corporate and household borrowing, was left at 3.0%, while the five-year LPR, a reference rate for mortgages, remained at 3.5%. Meanwhile, industrial production and retail sales grew at their fastest pace in six months, while the unemployment rate eased. New yuan loans continued to rise in September, though they remained below the level recorded a year earlier, as weak household demand weighed on lending amid slowing economic activity. Housing prices continued to decline in September, though the pace of contraction was the slowest in nine months, supported by government measures.

Japanese Shares Fall on Tech Weakness — The Nikkei 225 Index fell 1% to around 64,300 on Tuesday, approaching one-month lows as concerns over AI spending and rising borrowing costs continued to weigh on technology stocks. Japanese equities also diverged from Wall Street’s overnight recovery, led by chipmakers, as local companies remain highly vulnerable to AI-related selloffs. Notable decliners included Kioxia Holdings (-2.9%), SoftBank Group (-4.8%), Taiyo Yuden (-3.4%), Fujikura (-4.2%) and Tokyo Electron (-1.7%). Financial stocks also weakened, with Mitsubishi UFJ falling 1.5%, Mizuho Financial declining 2.8% and Sumitomo Mitsui slipping 1.6%. On monetary policy, Bank of Japan Governor Kazuo Ueda said underlying inflation was already nearing the central bank’s 2% target, reinforcing expectations of a potentially tighter policy stance. Meanwhile, investors are awaiting upcoming trade and inflation data for further indications of Japan’s economic outlook.

### 2026-10-20 08:23:04  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Stock Market #Importance 1

Australian Stocks Retreat from Near 5-Week High — Australian shares fell 85 points or 1% to 8,760 in early Tuesday trade, snapping a two-session rise as profit-taking set in after markets hit a near five-week top. Sentiment was also pressured by renewed churn in global tech stocks and doubts over AI-related spending. Locally, RBA Assistant Governor Kent noted that rising global neutral rates raise questions about whether policy is restrictive enough despite four hikes this year, tempering confidence in the current stance. Still, losses were partly offset by stronger-than-expected Q3 GDP and September activity data from China, aided by Golden Week demand. Tech, logistics, non-energy minerals, and financials drove the downside amid steep losses in heavyweights BHP Group (-2.1%) and Rio Tinto (-1.2%). The four big banks slumped between 1.5% and 2.7%, while other notable laggards included Northern Star Resources (-2.2%), Evolution Mining (-3.1%), and Qantas Airways (-1.7%). Traders now await China’s monthly lending rate fix later today.

【中信證券：光伏行業轉向高質量發展新階段】 中信證券指出，10月16日，商務部、科技部就修訂《中國禁止出口限制出口技術目錄》徵求意見，將光伏的多晶矽、矽片、電池三個環節的核心技術與關鍵裝置一併納入管制；同日工信部發布《光伏製造行業規範條件（2026年本）》徵求意見稿，全面轉向N型並收緊效率與能耗門檻。兩項政策一外一內，對外延緩海外產能擴張，對內加速落後過剩產能退出。中信證券認為光伏產業正由同質化價格競爭，轉向以技術、能耗為核心的高質量發展新階段。政策端透過提高技術指標與實施出口限制，壓縮低效產能的生存空間，具備效率與成本優勢的先進產能有望明顯受益。

### 2026-10-20 08:21:26  #公司 #市場 #國際

【散戶熱捧難擋破發：韓國資料中心企業 Elice 上市暴跌 32%】韓國資料中心基建企業 Elice 在韓國科技股為主的科斯達克市場上市，本次 IPO 募資 2010 億韓元（摺合 1.51 億美元），是該交易所今年規模最大的 IPO；上市後股價大幅下挫。

這家總部位於首爾的新一代雲服務商周二早盤股價最多下跌 32%，跌至 61400 韓元。公司 IPO 發行價定為每股 90500 韓元，處於推介價格區間上限，上市後總市值超過 1 萬億韓元。

全球多家同類企業接連遇挫，本次 Elice 的上市表現，是檢驗投資者對該板塊偏好的一次測試。媒體訊息稱，受借貸成本、激烈競爭以及 AI 鉅額投入的長期回報不確定性拖累估值，Firmus Grid 本月取消備受關注的悉尼上市計劃；Coreweave 股價自 5 月高點以來腰斬；知情人士表示 AirTrunk 推遲旗下不動產投資信託的 IPO 計劃；Nscale 的目標 IPO 估值也遭到投資者抵制。

Petra Capital Management 管理合夥人兼首席投資官Albert Yong表示：“Elice 上市之際，市場對 AI 基礎設施企業的情緒已經轉弱，在美國尤其明顯。AI 基建板塊市場情緒波動極大，股價走勢常常脫離基本面。”

Elice 本次 IPO 仍獲得韓國散戶投資者追捧，散戶申購保證金規模約達 10.3 萬億韓元。面向普通投資者的公開發行超額認購倍數超 400 倍。

### 2026-10-20 08:12:38  #MKT News #Important #Market Regions #Greater China

【China hog futures plunge; spot prices near cash cost, supply may decline after Q2 2027 if losses persist】China hog futures main 2701 contract extended losses yesterday, down 2.48% to near 10,000 yuan/ton, marking a record low since listing and a 64% decline from the peak of 28,400 yuan/ton. Nationwide average hog price fell below 10 yuan/kg. GUOTAI JUNAN SECURITIES said spot hog prices have traded near industry cash cost for six months. Key drivers: accelerated industry consolidation has left large, cash-rich producers delaying capacity cuts versus smallholders; recent improvements in breeding and feed-to-meat efficiency have concentrated a capacity dividend this year; when losses began in Q4 last year many producers kept output expecting a cycle turn, leaving supply pressure into Q4. Medium-term, if the sector remains loss-making into Q3, markets will trim forward spot-supply expectations and, with policy guidance, social and corporate capacity exits could accelerate; if losses persist into Q4 the sector could enter an outsized destocking phase, with supply likely to decline gradually after Q2 2027 and support a subsequent price recovery.

【涉案1400餘萬元！網警打掉一侵犯公民個人資訊犯罪團伙】“低息貸款”“秒批放款” 你是否收到過這種簡訊？而這看似普通的貸款推銷資訊，背後卻藏著一條侵犯公民個人資訊的黑色產業鏈，一旦你點選連結、撥打電話諮詢，極易掉入貸款“陷阱”，造成財產損失。近日，江西宜春公安網安部門重拳出擊，對一個藏匿於寫字樓內的侵犯公民個人資訊犯罪團伙開展集中統一收網行動，一舉端掉以羅某宇等人為首的犯罪團伙，當場抓獲涉案人員23名，查扣涉案電腦、手機等電子裝置70餘臺。經查，該團伙非法獲取大量公民個人資訊，藉助簡訊通道批次傳送虛假銀行貸款營銷簡訊，為下游非正規貸款機構引流，從中牟取非法利益，涉案金額1400餘萬元。目前，23名涉案人員均已被依法採取刑事強制措施，案件正在進一步偵辦中。

【網傳大閘蟹“全面滯銷”？記者調查最新行情】最近一段時間，網路上流傳著大閘蟹“全面滯銷”的說法，部分產區中小規格母蟹價格跌至5元一隻的訊息引發廣泛關注。在江蘇省無錫市朝陽農貿市場水產區，大閘蟹價格較去年同期實惠不少。12元就能拎走一隻二兩半的母蟹，有市民一口氣挑了十多隻。水產區經營戶表示，今年產量大、貨很多，大量上市價格就很低，我們按只賣的，像這種二兩五的之前是15元錢一隻，現在12元錢一隻，小規格的螃蟹都降了這麼多，大規格的螃蟹降得更多。經營戶的直觀感受是，價格下降是因為集中放量。有研究人員測算，2025年全國成蟹產量為93.9萬噸，2026年有望首次突破100萬噸。產量提升，並不意味著銷路受阻。蘇州市陽澄湖大閘蟹行業協會昨天（10月19日）釋出的公告指出，網傳“全面滯銷、價格腰斬”的說法混淆了產區邊界，正宗陽澄湖大閘蟹供需平穩，不存在庫存積壓、崩盤甩貨等情況。（央視）

Brent Steadies as Traders Monitor Mideast Flows — Brent prices held steady above $109 per barrel on Tuesday, maintaining recent gains as investors weighed escalating conflicts in the Middle East and Eastern Europe against signs of robust energy flows from the Persian Gulf. The Trump administration is also authorizing the sale of sanctioned oil tankers to enable them to transport crude legally, with the global “dark fleet” estimated at around 1,600 vessels. Meanwhile, Iran continued to assert control over the Strait of Hormuz, with maritime authorities reporting multiple attacks on commercial ships. Saudi Arabia and Iran-backed Houthi militants in Yemen also traded strikes, while Russia and Ukraine resumed attacks on energy infrastructure.

### 2026-10-20 07:30:06  #公司

【證券時報頭條評論：AI是助手不是替身 研報責任主體仍是分析師】生成式AI（人工智慧）近兩年在券商投研領域快速普及。從研報初稿、會議紀要到估值模型搭建，AI都已深度介入。效率提升的同時，合規風險也接踵而至：個別分析師將AI生成的資料和觀點未經核驗直接引入研報；更有甚者，AI編造的政策資訊、虛構的行業資料經分析師署名後發向市場，導致研報出現政策時間錯位、資料來源無法追溯等問題。中國證券業協會近日下發的《2025年證券公司釋出證券研究報告業務經營情況統計報告》，首次將“建立AI投研內容稽核內控機制”寫進行業規範，明確禁止分析師直接採信AI生成的未經核驗的資料、觀點、推演和研究結論。這一條文內容雖簡，但分量不輕。它重新界定了AI與分析師在投研鏈條中的位置：AI可以是輔助工具，但不能替代專業判斷；分析師可以借力技術，但必須對最終釋出的報告負責。技術本身是中性的。它既可以放大研究效率，也可以放大研究錯誤。唯有把AI的輔助角色擺正，把分析師的守門責任壓實，把機構的內控閉環建全，技術紅利才能真正轉化為研究質量。

### 2026-10-20 07:21:44  #其他

【經濟日報：“次拋衣”走紅不應加劇低價內卷】近來，一種被年輕人稱作“次拋衣”的服裝在社交平臺悄然刷屏。不少人專門為了一次旅行、一個場景買下服裝，穿一兩次、拍完照片便閒置或轉手。應當看到，“次拋衣”的流行有其現實土壤。隨著居民收入水平提高和消費觀念變遷，年輕一代對服裝的需求正從“耐穿百搭”向“場景細分”延展，職場、日常、旅拍的穿搭邏輯完全不同。社交平臺把“出片”變成一種社交貨幣，年輕人為審美、情緒乃至一段專屬記憶付費，這是消費升級程序中的自然切片，不必急於貼上“浪費”的標籤。從這個意義上說，“次拋衣”的走紅恰是我國服裝市場活力仍在、消費需求持續細分的佐證。然而，繁榮表象之下的產業隱憂也不容忽視。需客觀看待的是，服裝業規模以上企業利潤承壓是原材料成本波動、出口需求分流、線下渠道修復不及預期等多重因素疊加的結果，但“次拋衣”催生的低價內卷，無疑進一步放大了行業困境。消費是最終需求，也是人民對美好生活需要的直接體現。“次拋衣”的流行，是這代年輕人審美表達的一個出口，也是中國服裝行業向高質量發展轉型必須邁過的一道坎。衣服可以“次拋”，但產業的價值不能“次拋”，品牌的根基不能“次拋”，發展的質量更不能“次拋”。唯有讓流量歸位、價值迴歸、品牌向上，才能讓服裝這一傳統優勢產業，走出低價內卷的內耗，走上高質量發展的長遠軌道。

### 2026-10-20 07:11:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Indonesia #Foreign Direct Investment YoY #Importance 1

Indonesia FDI Rises the Most in Nearly 2 Years — Foreign direct investment (FDI) into Indonesia, excluding the financial and oil and gas sectors, surged 27.5% year-on-year to a record high of IDR 270.2 trillion (US$14.99 billion) in Q3 2026, following a 27.4% jump in Q2, marking the strongest growth since Q4 2024. The increase came despite persistent global economic uncertainty amid the conflict in the Middle East. The base metals industry attracted the largest inflows, while Hong Kong was the top source of investment (US$5.2 billion), followed by Singapore (US$5.1 billion), the US (US$1.7 billion), China (US$1.1 billion), and South Korea (US$0.8 billion). Meanwhile, total direct investment, including domestic sources, reached IDR 528.7 trillion, up 7.6% year-on-year, creating more than 745,634 jobs. FDI accounted for 51.1% of total investment in Q3.

8、蘋果公司確認將於10月28日舉行新品釋出會，屆時計劃推出公司首款觸屏MacBook。這場主題為“Proandtell”的活動將在洛杉磯舉行。
9、高盛主經紀商業務部門的資料顯示，對沖基金以至少十年來最快速度減持科技股。截至10月15日的五個交易日，對沖基金削減科技股倉位的幅度創高盛2016年開始統計以來最大。

NZX 50 Little Changed in Morning Trade — The NZX 50 was almost flat, trading around 13,657 in Tuesday morning deals, after losses in the previous two sessions, as gains in the tech and healthcare sectors were offset by declines in utilities and real estate. Traders were cautious ahead of the release of Q3 inflation data due this week, which could guide the RBNZ's interest rate decision. Caution also stemmed from rising oil prices, raising concerns about inflation and interest rate hikes amid escalating tensions in the Middle East. The broader index attempted to rebound from its lowest level since September 16, touched a day earlier, tracking an upbeat session on Wall Street overnight, supported by a rebound in the tech sector. F&C Investment rose 1.8%, Infratil climbed 1.3%, Fisher & Paykel gained 0.6%, and Mainfreight increased 0.5%, while Mercury NZ dropped 0.7%, and ANZ Group and A2 Milk each fell 0.5%.

【U.S. reportedly delays approval to transfer "Patriot" interceptors to Ukraine; Kyiv says stocks nearly exhausted】The Washington Post reported Monday that the Trump administration has not approved transfers of "Patriot" interceptor missiles to Ukraine for defense against Russian missiles and drones. At least some interceptors were to be supplied by U.S.-friendly countries but require approval by U.S. Defense Secretary Pete Hegseth because the munitions are U.S.-made and subject to retransfer rules, leaving final authority with the U.S. A Ukrainian official said Kyiv’s interceptor inventory has fallen to only a handful. Officials said Hegseth has not formally rejected transfer requests but has not completed required approvals; he continues to sign off on some U.S. aid to Ukraine. The report noted Trump has recently adopted a more Russia-friendly stance and increased criticism of Ukraine.

Brazilian Stocks Close Lower Amid Election Uncertainty — The Ibovespa edged 0.2% lower to close at 205,079 on Monday, erasing earlier gains as credit-sensitive stocks ended mixed. Brazilian assets traded without a clear direction as investors remained cautious at the start of the week ahead of the presidential runoff. Polls by BTG/Nexus and AtlasIntel showed declining support for Flávio Bolsonaro and rising support for Lula, although Bolsonaro remained ahead in both surveys. The BTG/Nexus poll indicated a statistical tie, while AtlasIntel showed Bolsonaro’s lead exceeding the margin of error. Although Bolsonaro has yet to present detailed policy plans, investors view his candidacy as more likely to bring fiscal reforms, including spending cuts, which are seen as important to lowering interest rates. Financials closed mixed, with Itaú down 0.4% and Bradesco shedding 1.3%, while BB gained 1.3% and B3 rose 1.8%. Utilities were mostly lower, with Axia falling 1.3%. Petrobras also weighed on the index, dropping 2.8%.

TSX Slips Despite Softer-Than-Expected Inflation — The S&P/TSX Composite Index fell 0.7% to close at 35,929 on Monday as investors assessed fresh inflation data and the outlook for Bank of Canada (BoC) policy. Canada’s annual inflation rate rose to 3.1% in September, its highest level in four months, driven by higher gasoline prices, but came in below market expectations of 3.2%. Despite the downside surprise, concerns about the inflation outlook persisted, with policymakers flagging upside risks to prices. BoC Governor Tiff Macklem said on Saturday that the central bank expects to raise its quarterly inflation forecast significantly this month, following hawkish remarks last week about energy-driven price pressures. Oil prices remained elevated amid attacks on tankers in the Strait of Hormuz, keeping inflation risks in focus. Major banks declined, with RBC down 0.9%, while BMO, CIBC and National Bank each shed 0.8%. Mining stocks also fell as gold prices moved lower.

【DOE moves to convert sanctioned tankers into compliant vessels to carry unsanctioned crude】U.S. Energy Secretary Wright said the Trump administration is pushing to reclassify some sanctioned tankers via an approval process so they can operate as compliant vessels. Investors plan to buy idle tankers and refurbish them to transport crude not subject to sanctions. Wright emphasized this is not a lift of sanctions on ships carrying Russian, Iranian or other sanctioned crude; the aim is to bring those vessels into legal operation and ensure they no longer transport sanctioned oil. He estimated the global sanctioned shadow fleet at about 1,600 vessels and said the plan could add tens or even hundreds of legally operating tankers.

### 2026-10-20 04:20:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Argentina #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Argentina Trade Surplus Widens in September — Argentina’s trade surplus widened to $2.98 billion in September 2026, up $2.04 billion from the same month of 2025. Exports totaled $10.04 billion, increasing 23.5% year-on-year. All categories recorded higher export values than in September 2025, led by fuels and energy, which surged 89.1%. Manufactured industrial goods rose 17.5%, manufactured agricultural goods increased 16.8%, and primary products grew 9.2%. Imports totaled $7.06 billion, down 1.8%. The economic-use category with the largest percentage increase was parts and accessories for capital goods, which rose 11.9%. Passenger vehicle imports increased 3.5%, while capital goods declined 10.4% and consumer goods fell 2.5%.

Brent Tops $104 on Supply Concerns — Brent crude prices rose above $109 a barrel on Monday, reaching a more than one-month high after surging 5% last week, as renewed tensions in the Middle East heightened concerns over supply disruptions. A senior Iranian security official warned on Sunday that Tehran had prepared a new response plan in the event of renewed US-Israeli attacks, while maritime authorities reported several separate attacks on commercial vessels around the Arabian Peninsula. Meanwhile, Saudi Arabia intercepted several missiles over the Red Sea on Friday, including one headed toward the Yanbu oil export terminal. Iran-allied Houthi militants in Yemen also launched repeated attacks on Saudi Arabia last week. The seven-day moving average of oil flows through the Strait of Hormuz fell to just 8.5 million barrels per day, down from an estimated 20 million barrels per day the previous week.

### 2026-10-20 02:18:40  #國際

【英偉達于于利希超級計算中心完成創紀錄的容錯量子計算模擬】英偉達研究人員正利用歐洲最大超級計算機——建於德國於利希超級計算中心的JUPITER——進行一項破紀錄的容錯量子計算模擬。此次104量子位元模擬是迄今為止對容錯量子計算系統最全面的模擬，此前沒有任何模擬能以如此完整的細節對量子處理器進行建模。該模擬驗證了一種量子計算方法，該方法所需的物理量子位元數量比此前的方法減少90%，從而加速了量子計算在藥物發現、金融建模和材料科學等領域實現實際應用的程序。JUPITER的模擬為量子產業提供了關於噪聲對容錯量子計算影響的可行洞察，使研究人員能夠在構建硬體之前最佳化設計，將實現可用量子計算機的路徑縮短數年。

【高盛：對沖基金以至少十年來最快速度減持科技股】高盛主經紀商業務部門的資料顯示，對沖基金以至少十年來最快速度減持科技股。截至10月15日的五個交易日，對沖基金削減科技股倉位的幅度創高盛2016年開始統計以來最大。與此同時，高盛全球銀行與市場部門的一份報告顯示，總交易活動下降速度創近三個月來最快。對沖基金在全球科技股大跌之際進行了拋售。隨著AI主題交易在全球範圍內退潮，納斯達克100指數上週下跌5.5%，創2025年4月以來最大跌幅。在高盛資料涵蓋的這五個交易日中，科技股為MSCI全球指數中表現最差的板塊。高盛資料顯示，全球股票淨賣出資金流達到過去一年資金流的兩個標準差。資訊科技板塊的淨賣出幅度遙遙領先，資訊科技板塊淨賣出最為突出，而半導體板塊賣出金額最大。不過，對沖基金仍持有大量科技股倉位。報告稱，科技板塊佔全球Prime持倉的23.5%，對該板塊的淨配置仍處於過去五年的第90百分位。但對沖基金已經變得更加謹慎。淨槓桿率降至一年低點48.6%，這一指標衡量多頭倉位超過空頭倉位的程度。

DAX Closes in the Red — Frankfurt’s DAX 40 closed about 1.2% down at 25,320 on Monday, tracking broader weakness across European markets, pressured by rising bond yields and elevated oil prices. Violent weekend protests in France raised concerns about political and fiscal stability, prompting investors to reassess sovereign debt risks and pushing government bond yields higher. At the same time, persistent geopolitical risks kept oil prices elevated. Meanwhile, the earnings season continued to draw attention. Most sectors faced pressure, particularly banks, utilities, industrials and insurers. Deutsche Bank and Commerzbank were among the worst performers, dropping 3.2% and 2.7%, respectively. RWE (-3%) and Bayer (-2.4%) followed suit. Rheinmetall fell 2.2%, weighed down by concerns over the costs of its expansion plans. The defense group took a small stake in Rock Tech Lithium and its German lithium processing project in a deal worth €138 million.

### 2026-10-20 00:08:54  #Trading Economics #Markets #Euro Area #Stock Market #Importance 1

European Stocks Pull Back — European stocks closed sharply lower on Monday as fresh civil unrest added to local political uncertainty, triggering selloffs in the bond markets of major Eurozone members. The Euro STOXX 50 fell 1% to 6,141 and the STOXX Europe 600 lost 0.9% to 639. Protests across France and Italy against rising living costs escalated into clashes with local security forces, pressuring the outlook for the countries' respective budgets to pass smoothly ahead of their elections next year, at a time where rising yields across the Eurozone had already triggered concerns of contagion in the bloc's sovereign bond market. Banks therefore led the losses, with Deutsche Bank, BNP Paribas, Santander, UniCredit, and Intesa Sanpaolo dropping over 2%. Industrials were also down, with Airbus and Rheinmetall dropping close to 3%. Meanwhile, Hermès and SAP were relatively close to the flatline ahead of their earnings this week.

Italian Stocks Drop to Five-Month Low — Milan’s FTSE MIB plunged 1.5% to 49,086 on Monday, hitting a five-month low as escalating protests across Europe dampened investor sentiment. Mass protests in France escalated over the weekend, while protests over the rising cost of living in Milan also turned disorderly, leading to clashes with police. Sovereign bond yields surged across Europe, pressuring bank balance sheets and weighing on financial stocks. Banco BPM fell 3.7% after Jefferies cut its price target, while Unipol dropped 3.2% following antitrust approval for its control of BPER Banca (-2.2%). UniCredit and Intesa Sanpaolo each lost 2.2%. Stellantis slid 3.2%, tracking broader European auto-sector losses, with Equita’s downward revision of its third-quarter earnings estimates adding to the pressure. Luxury stocks declined amid early store closures in Italy and looting in France following protests, with Brunello Cucinelli down 1.3% and Moncler falling 1.2%. Defense stocks also underperformed, led by Leonardo (-2.2%).

### 2026-10-20 00:02:47  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Stock Market #Importance 0

CAC 40 Falls as Fiscal and Political Risks Mount — The CAC 40 fell 1.1% to close at 7,868 on Monday amid civil unrest and deepening political turmoil. Calls for higher public spending come as France faces mounting pressure to rein in its growing debt burden, complicating efforts to address social grievances without worsening its fiscal position. The protests unfolded as French lawmakers debated the government’s proposed 2027 budget, which includes €43 billion in measures aimed at reducing the deficit. The unrest also comes at a critical juncture ahead of the presidential election. A recent poll showed far-right leader Marine Le Pen and hard-left leader Jean-Luc Mélenchon gaining ground ahead of the election. Both candidates are viewed as not committed to fiscal restraint. Losses were broad-based, led by financial stocks. Crédit Agricole fell 2.6%, Société Générale shed 3.4%, and BNP Paribas lost 2.5%. EssilorLuxottica dropped 1.5% ahead of its earnings release, as Morgan Stanley cut its price target amid governance concerns.

【Commerzbank: German debt concerns converging with France】Commerzbank economist Joerg Kraemer said in a report that Germany’s debt problem is increasingly converging with France’s. He said Berlin has responded to energy-price shocks with public spending, including two fuel tax cuts that disproportionately benefited wealthier households and reduced incentives to save energy. Against a fragile governing coalition and policy divisions, Kraemer expects Germany’s budget deficit to widen to about 4% of GDP this year—near France’s 5%—and called for prioritizing public finances and raising competitiveness to support growth.

FTSE 100 Falls by Over 1% — The FTSE 100 fell more than 1% on Monday, ending a three-session winning streak as banking and mining stocks led the decline. Barclays dropped 3.5% after reports that the BoE was investigating whether its traders had misrepresented illiquid private-credit loans to understate their risk. The news weighed on the wider banking sector, with HSBC and Lloyds Banking Group losing more than 2%, NatWest falling nearly 3% and Standard Chartered declining 2.5%. Mining shares also came under pressure, as Anglo American fell 4%, Glencore slid more than 3.5%, and Rio Tinto lost almost 2%. Aerospace and defence stocks weakened, with Rolls-Royce and BAE Systems each down around 2%. Investors are now preparing for UK September inflation and retail sales data due this week. Attention is also turning to Finance Minister John Healey’s first budget speech, scheduled for next week. Meanwhile, oil prices eased from five-month highs as traders assessed Middle East tensions and regional supply resilience.

### 2026-10-19 23:13:46  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Lumber Drops Toward Two-Year Low — Lumber futures fell below $527 per thousand board feet, retreating toward a two-year low amid persistent weakness in the US housing market. Homebuilder confidence has fallen to its lowest level in 14 years, as high mortgage rates, labor shortages and high construction costs continue to weigh on the sector. Mortgage rates have climbed to 7.4%, their highest level in three years, raising borrowing costs, dampening housing demand and weighing on prices. On the other hand, trade tensions with Canada continue to constrain lumber availability and pressuring prices. Although the US Department of Commerce lowered antidumping and countervailing duties on Canadian softwood lumber to 24.75% from 35.16%, a separate 10% US tariff brings total duties to 34.75%, limiting the potential for a supply recovery. The US Lumber Coalition backed the decision, as forestry groups have long accused Canada of dumping lumber in the US. Conversely, the NAHB has called for a pause on building material tariffs.

Baltic Dry Index Breaks 10-Day Losing Run — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, snapped its long ten-day losing sreak on Monday, rising about 1.4% to 2,546 points, mainly due to a rebound in the larger vessel segment. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes, including iron ore and coal, advanced by 3.6% to 3,365 points, after ten consecutive sessions of declines, partly tracking higher iron ore prices amid stronger crude steel production and better-than-expected economic growth in China. At the same time, the supramax index rose by 0.2% to 1,868 points. Meanwhile, the panamax index, which usually carries 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, fell 1.2% to 2,286 points.

【世界模型評測平臺WorldOracle釋出】近日，全球首個以真實執行為標尺的動作驅動世界模型評測平臺WorldOracle正式釋出，並向開發者開放評測提交。該專案由智元、NVIDIA、普林斯頓大學、得克薩斯大學奧斯汀分校、多倫多大學的研究人員聯合完成。WorldOracle主要用於評估世界模型對機器人動作執行結果的預測準確性。與傳統側重影片生成質量的評測方式不同，該平臺以機器人真實運算元據為參照，將模型生成的預測影片與真實執行過程進行對比，重點檢驗模型對物理互動結果的預測能力。目前，WorldOracle已收錄335條真實操作軌跡，涵蓋水果抓取、毛巾懸掛、倒水、抽屜開合等任務，其中約40%的互動片段包含失敗或次優操作。

Canada’s 10-Year Yield Rises on Hawkish BoC Bets — Canada’s 10-year government bond yield rose to around 3.90% in October, its highest level in nearly two weeks, following the release of September inflation data. The annual inflation rate climbed to 3.1%, its highest level in four months, driven by higher gasoline prices, but came in below market expectations of 3.2%. While rising energy prices pushed headline inflation higher, underlying measures remained subdued, with CPI-median slowing to 1.9% and CPI-trim easing to 1.8% in September. Despite the downside surprise, the outlook for Bank of Canada policy remained tilted toward tighter conditions, as policymakers flagged upside risks to inflation. Governor Tiff Macklem said on Saturday that the central bank expects to raise its quarterly inflation forecast significantly this month, following hawkish remarks last week about energy-driven price pressures.

South Africa 10-Year Bond Yield Hovers at 1-Month Low — South Africa’s 10-year government bond yield was around 8.90%, near one-month lows, as investors continued to seek attractive returns and grew optimistic over the country’s fiscal outlook. Markets are looking ahead to Finance Minister Enoch Godongwana’s mid-term budget update on October 21, amid hopes that stronger revenue collection, particularly from mining, and fewer bailouts to state-owned companies will help reduce the budget deficit and put government debt on a more sustainable path. A clearer commitment to fiscal discipline could also bolster investor confidence and increase the likelihood of sovereign credit rating upgrades. However, weak economic growth, elevated domestic fuel costs and potential spending pressures ahead of the November local government elections remain challenges. Investors will also scrutinize September’s inflation report, with the latest figures expected to show a further acceleration in price growth.

【中原證券鄧淑斌：資本市場底層邏輯深刻轉變 “十五五”改革築牢長期向好根基】10月19日，證監會主席吳清在2026金融街論壇年會開幕式上發表主旨講話。中原證券首席經濟學家、證券研究所所長鄧淑斌解讀認為，講話明確資本市場執行底層邏輯已發生深刻變化、中國資產配置價值更加凸顯，圍繞穩市場、引入長錢、嚴監管、擴大開放、建好多層次資本市場，將“十五五”中長期規劃轉化為可落地制度工具、產品創新和監管舉措。鄧淑斌分析，吳清講話釋放出七大積極訊號，一是改革堅持謀長遠、抓當下，邊部署邊落地，政策工具箱持續啟用，著力穩定市場預期、增強內在韌性。二是改革主線轉向投融資雙向協調，構建投融資良性正向迴圈。三是中長期資金入市邁入產品化、機制化落地階段，壯大資本市場“穩定器”。四是法治化建設提速，補齊監管短板，提升市場公信力。五是高水平對外開放穩步推進、開放安全並重。六是多層次資本市場板塊差異化功能持續強化，搭建覆蓋企業全生命週期的股權融資體系。七是風險治理體系與時俱進。鄧淑斌還認為，包容性改革增量集中於發行融資制度、上市公司監管法治和激勵約束機制，將形成“篩選上市—提質增效—價值兌現—吸引科創與資本”閉環。

US Homebuilder Sentiment Lowest Since 2012 — The NAHB/Wells Fargo Housing Market Index (HMI), which tracks US homebuilder confidence in the market for newly built single-family homes, fell for a second consecutive month to 29 in October 2026, the lowest since June 2012, from 32 in September and below forecasts of 32. Current sales conditions fell two points to 33, also a 14-year low. Sales expectations in the next six months dropped five points to 32, the lowest since November 2022 while traffic of prospective buyers declined five points to 18. Also, 43% of builders cut prices in October, the highest share during the 2020-2026 period. The average price cut remained at 6% for the seventh consecutive month. “Housing remains squeezed by supply-side constraints and significant affordability challenges. Demand persists, but higher mortgage rates, elevated construction costs and weaker consumer confidence are keeping more buyers on the sidelines”, according to Robert Dietz, NAHB chief economist.

【Qatar plans power aid for Lebanon including two plants and 20-year LNG deal】A document seen by Refinitiv shows Qatar plans to build two power plants in Lebanon within three years and sign a 20-year LNG supply agreement as part of a proposal to revive Lebanon's struggling power sector. The draft, titled National Integrated Energy Investment Plan, covers gas-fired generation, LNG supply and regasification, renewables, battery storage and regional power interconnection. The plan is designed to attract private capital or project financing without upfront Lebanese public budget outlays; payment obligations and guarantees remain to be negotiated. Lebanon's state utility currently supplies only a few hours of electricity per day, with the balance met by costly private diesel generators. Qatar's prime minister visited last week and pledged support. The blueprint needs approval from both governments and may be revised; if approvals proceed, implementation is expected to take three years.

### 2026-10-19 22:02:15  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Currency #Importance 1

Canadian Dollar Near One-Month High — The Canadian dollar strengthened to around 1.41 per US dollar in October, reaching a near one-month high following the release of September inflation data. Canada’s annual inflation rate rose to 3.1%, its highest level in four months, driven by higher gasoline prices, but came in below market expectations of 3.2%. While rising energy prices pushed headline inflation higher, underlying measures remained subdued, with CPI-median slowing to 1.9% and CPI-trim easing to 1.8% in September. Despite the downside surprise, the outlook for Bank of Canada policy remained tilted toward tighter conditions, as policymakers flagged upside risks to inflation. Governor Tiff Macklem said on Saturday that the central bank expects to raise its quarterly inflation forecast significantly this month, following hawkish remarks last week about energy-driven price pressures. Expectations of a narrowing US-Canada interest-rate differential have provided further support for the loonie.

### 2026-10-19 21:53:40  #國際

【被楊立昆質疑，馬斯克“沒繃住”】從馬斯克和楊立昆在海外社交平臺上的互動記錄來看，兩人的爭吵發生在過去的這個週末。馬斯克10月16日在自己的賬號上釋出了一段特斯拉“飛行”汽車的宣傳片後，曾榮獲2018年圖靈獎，還被一些美國媒體譽為“AI教父”的美國知名電腦科學家楊立昆（Yann LeCun，法裔），曾在馬斯克的帖文下留言，問這款車是不是用過氧化氫驅動實現飛行的。楊立昆後來解釋說，他是看到馬斯克的汽車在起飛時產生的大量蒸汽，所以好奇馬斯克用的是不是早在上世紀70年代就已經被用於噴氣動力裝置的過氧化氫。

在得知特斯拉官方披露該車使用壓縮空氣驅動後，楊立昆又在馬斯克的帖文下補了一段留言，表示如果馬斯克的車能“飛”是因為使用了壓縮空氣驅動，那麼這隻能證明這款車“完全沒有實用價值”，因為壓縮空氣“比衝很低”，能維持有效推力的時間很短。“所以這輛車只能是一個空心輕質外殼，裡面裝大型壓縮氣罐。當個趣味演示還行，完全沒有實用價值。”楊立昆寫道。他還在帖文中表示，馬斯克本人應該很清楚這點。

面對楊立昆的質疑，馬斯克顯然有些“沒繃住”。他留言回懟楊立昆說：我是朝你大致的方向排了個氣。

雖然馬斯克的這番回應立刻引發馬斯克支持者對楊立昆的大量圍攻——其中有人認為這位科學家不懂創新，還有人認為他不懂幽默，是那種給魔術師拆臺的“蠢貨”，但楊立昆也沒有示弱，而是繼續回覆馬斯克說：“排氣確實是你的車能飛起來的方式，只是希望味道沒有那麼臭。”目前，馬斯克未再回應楊立昆。（環球時報）

【李強會見白俄羅斯總理圖爾欽】10月19日下午，國務院總理李強在杜尚別出席上海合作組織成員國政府首腦（總理）理事會第二十五次會議期間會見白俄羅斯總理圖爾欽。李強指出，中方願同白方加強發展戰略對接，進一步發揮兩國政府間合作機制作用，充分釋放中白服務貿易和投資協定的積極效應，共同辦好“中白工業合作年”，深化汽車產業、數字經濟、人工智慧、綠色經濟等領域合作。中方願進口白方更多優質產品，支援中國企業赴白投資興業，希望白方為企業提供公開、透明、可預期的營商環境。雙方要擴大文化、旅遊、青年、媒體、地方等領域交流合作，讓中白友好更加深入人心。近年來，一些霸權主義、強權政治的行為愈演愈烈。中方願同白方加強在地區和國際事務中的溝通協調，密切在上合組織、金磚國家等機制內協作，促進多邊主義和自由貿易，捍衛兩國共同利益，維護國際公平正義。（新華社）

Ibovespa Rises Following Presidential Debate and New Election Polls — The Ibovespa rose nearly 1% on Monday, approaching 207,000, as investors assessed the first presidential debate between Flávio Bolsonaro and President Lula, held on Sunday night. Polls released on Monday by BTG/Nexus and AtlasIntel showed a decline in voting intentions for Flávio and an increase for Lula, although the senator remained ahead in both surveys. The BTG/Nexus poll showed a statistical tie, while AtlasIntel put Flávio’s lead beyond the margin of error. Although Bolsonaro has yet to present detailed policy plans, investors view him as more likely to pursue fiscal reforms, including spending cuts, which are seen as essential to lowering interest rates. Financial stocks mostly advanced, with Itaú, Itaúsa, and BB each gaining around 1%. Utilities also rose, with Axia up more than 1%. Other notable gainers included Rede D’Or, up 2%, and Embraer, which added 1%. Meanwhile, Petrobras fell 1% as the oil rally stalled.

### 2026-10-19 21:24:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Africa #Currency #Importance 0

South African Rand at Near 3-Week High — The South African rand traded around 16.4 per USD, its strongest level since late September, amid a broadly stable US dollar and optimism ahead of the upcoming budget statement. Investors are turning their attention to Finance Minister Enoch Godongwana’s medium-term budget policy statement due on October 21, amid expectations of stronger tax revenue collection, a narrowing fiscal deficit and a more sustainable debt trajectory. Progress on these fronts could strengthen confidence in South Africa’s public finances and support further sovereign credit rating upgrades. Still, record-high fuel prices, weak economic growth and pressure to increase public spending ahead of the November 4 local government elections pose challenges to sustained fiscal improvement. Attention will also be on September inflation and retail sales data for further insight into price pressures and the overall health of the South African economy.

### 2026-10-19 21:23:54  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

US 10-Year Treasury Yield Edges Up — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note edged up to 5.1% on Monday, following a nearly 15bps decline the previous week, as traders continued to weigh domestic inflation and interest rate expectations against broader geopolitical developments. Investors are currently pricing in an nearly 83% probability that the Fed will leave the federal funds rate unchanged next week, while the odds of a 25bps rate hike in December stand at around 75%. Speaking at the IMF’s annual meetings in Bangkok on Friday, Fed Chair Warsh refrained from signaling the outcome of this month’s FOMC meeting, noting that hotter-than-expected inflation had not altered his assessment of the US economy. His remarks helped reinforce market expectations that the Fed would hold rates steady. Both CPI and PPI reports released last week came in hotter than expected. Oil prices edged slightly lower on Monday but remained elevated.

TSX Futures Fall Amid Hawkish BoC Outlook — Canadian stock market futures moved lower on Monday as investors assessed fresh inflation data. Canada’s annual inflation rate reached 3.1% in September, its highest level in four months, driven by higher gasoline prices, but came in below market expectations of 3.2%. ### 2026-10-19 21:03:59  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Wheat Holds at 2-Month Low — Wheat hovered around $6.6 per bushel, the lowest in two months and more than 13% below the 3-1/2-year high of $7.67 reached on August 28, as rising Russian export volumes and expectations of a strong Australian harvest weighed on the market. Traders have largely overlooked renewed Black Sea shipping attacks, focusing instead on Russia’s growing use of Baltic ports after disruptions forced southern terminals to suspend much of their activity. Rusagrotrans expects Russian wheat exports to reach 3 million tonnes in October, while other analysts have also revised forecasts higher, reflecting the pace of shipments. Meanwhile, early crop assessments in Victoria suggest Australia could produce a substantially larger wheat harvest, potentially easing supply pressures for buyers in Southeast Asia and the Middle East. However, dry conditions are limiting yields in parts of northeastern Australia, and renewed Russian-Ukrainian attacks have clouded prospects for restoring Black Sea exports.

【振宏股份：擬投資17億元建設年產15萬噸高階裝備配套用鍛件及板材專案】10月19日，振宏股份釋出公告，公司擬在江蘇江陰臨港經濟開發區投資建設年產15萬噸高階裝備配套用鍛件及板材專案（以下簡稱“本專案”）。本專案總投資約17億元（最終投資金額以專案建設實際投入為準），投資內容主要包括：取得約200畝土地使用權、建設生產車間、變電站及相應公輔設施等，新購置各類生產及配套裝置125臺（套），覆蓋生產全流程。滿足風電、化工、船舶、核電、航空航天等高階裝備領域的鍛件/板材生產需求，可支撐年產15萬噸高階裝備配套用鍛件及板材批次生產。本專案計劃於2026年底開工建設，2028年底竣工投產，並於2030年底達產。專案建成達產後，可形成年產15萬噸高階裝備配套用鍛件及板材產能，產品主要應用於新能源、船舶、航空航天、核電等領域。

### 2026-10-19 20:40:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Inflation Rate #Importance 3

Canada Inflation Rises Less than Expected — The headline inflation rate in Canada rose to 3.1% in September of 2026 from 3.0% in the previous month, the highest reading since the two-year high of 3.2% in May, but below the market consensus of 3.2%. Prices accelerated for gasoline (27.8% vs 22.8% in August), tracking the recent surge in crude oil prices as the war in the Middle East prompted the US and Iran to tighten their blockade of oil and fuel tankers crossing the Persian Gulf. Excluding gasoline, the CPI rose by 2.2% annually, slowing from the 2.4% increase in the previous month. In the meantime, shelter costs also climbed (1.8% vs 1.5%) as the surge in longer-term bond yields pushed homeowners' and mortgage insurance up 4.9% annually. On the other hand, inflation softened for food (2.2% vs 2.8%) due to a sharp slowdown in food purchased from grocery stores (1.7% vs 2.8%). The core inflation rates tracked by the BoC remained below the 2% threshold, with the trimmed-mean rate at 1.8%, below expectations of 1.9%.

### 2026-10-19 20:24:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kazakhstan #Monthly GDP YoY #Importance 0

Kazakhstan Economy Expands 4.8% in January-September — Kazakhstan’s economy grew by 4.8% year-on-year in January–September 2026, exceeding the Economy Minister’s initial estimate of 4.2%, but slowing from 4.9% in the first eight months of the year. According to Economy Minister Zhumangarin, the deceleration was primarily driven by an 8.3% decline in oil production over the period, reflecting the country’s heavy reliance on mineral and oil exports. Oil production began to recover in August, returning to previous levels following a July decline caused by disruptions to exports through the Caspian Pipeline Consortium. Mining and quarrying ouput continued to decline at a steady rate of 4.3%. Meanwhile, growth also slowed in construction (11.5% vs 15.6% in January-August) and transportation and storage (7.2% vs 7.3%).

US Futures Gain on Tech Strength — US stock futures were mixed on Monday as a rebound in heavyweight AI infrastructure stocks offset muted movement in broader sectors. Tech gains lifted the S&P 500 by 0.5% and the Nasdaq 100 by 1.2%, while the Dow inched below the flatline. The advance contrasted with tech weakness last week, when underwhelming financial results from Anthropic and signs of debt stress among cloud providers, especially CoreWeave, dented demand for speculative positions in the sector. Dip buyers lifted AMD, Micron, Lam Research, and SanDisk by over 4% each premarket. The credit stress in the sector also eased concerns that the US Treasury would be further crowded out of capital by primary dealers, lowering longer-term yields. Banks were muted following their earnings week, while Meta and Tesla inched down ahead of their earnings results this week.

### 2026-10-19 19:34:04  #Trading Economics #Markets #Switzerland #Currency #Importance 0

Swiss Franc Stays Weak as Rate Gap Widens — The Swiss franc strenghened to near 0.82 per USD, but remains close to its weakest level since May 2025 amid a widening interest rate differential with the US and selling pressure from carry trades. Expectations that borrowing costs in the US will remain elevated while Swiss borrowing costs remain at the world's lowest has added to the Swiss currency's appeal as a funding source for carry trades. The selling pressure of franc-loans weakens the currency as traders seek other higher-yielding currencies. The Swiss National Bank has kept its policy rate unchanged at 0% for over a year, with Chair Martin Schlegel stating no adjustment was currently needed as inflation remains stable. While economists expect rates to remain unchanged through 2027, markets are anticipating a hike in early 2027. Policymakers have also scaled back threats of currency intervention. However, growing safe-haven demand amid concerns over Europe's fiscal position provides an offsetting pressure.

Israel Jobless Rate Edges Up — Israel’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate edged up to 3.0% in September 2026 from 2.8% in the previous month. Meanwhile, the employment rate edged higher to 60.3% from 60.0% in August, as the number of employed persons increased by 24.5 thousand from the previous month and 54.1 thousand from a year earlier to 4,421.8 thousand. Italy's BTP Yield Climbs Amid Social Unrest — Italy’s 10-year BTP yield rose above 4.55%, hovering near its highest level in three years, amid growing social unrest across Europe and concerns over the continent's fiscal outlook. Mass protests in France escalated over the weekend, while protests against the rising cost of living in Milan also turned disorderly, leading to clashes with police. The unrest has added to concerns over Europe’s fiscal stability ahead of next year’s elections, including in Italy, whose debt burden is expected to surpass Greece’s this year as the eurozone’s most indebted country. Its 2027 draft budget deficit is seen rising to 3.4% of GDP in 2027 from an estimated 2.9% this year. It includes planned contributions from banks and insurers to ease fiscal pressures, alongside potential tax relief for households and higher defense and energy spending. Premier Meloni is seeking an agreement on the proposed contributions, with a summit of the governing coalition scheduled for Monday to discuss the approach.

Gilts have therefore remained relatively resilient.

Albania Trade Deficit Narrows in September — Albania’s trade deficit narrowed to ALL 47.3 billion in September 2026 from ALL 47.8 billion in the same month a year earlier. Exports rose 14.8% to ALL 31.2 billion, lifted by higher shipments of minerals, fuels, and electricity (+12.8 pp), construction materials and metals (+3.4 pp), and machinery, equipment, and spare parts (+1.8 pp), partly offset by lower sales of textiles and footwear (-5.5 pp). Exports increased the most to Kosovo (+22.5%) and Greece (+13.9%). Meanwhile, imports rose 4.8% to ALL 78.6 billion, driven largely by higher purchases of minerals, fuels, and electricity (+3.2 pp), construction materials and metals (+0.5 pp), and food, beverages, and tobacco (+0.5 pp). Imports increased the most from Turkey (+6.8%) and Greece (+3.8%). The gap narrowed as exports rose faster than imports. During the January–September period, exports totalled ALL 278.9 billion, up from ALL 260.2 billion, while imports reached ALL 690.3 billion, up from ALL 656.9 billion.

【Qatari LNG tanker makes rare Red Sea transit toward Poland, exposing cargo to Houthi attack risk】A tanker loaded with Qatari LNG in August is making a rare northbound transit of the Red Sea en route to Poland, ship-tracking data show. The route traverses waters at high risk of Houthi attack; passage shortens delivery time to Europe compared with rerouting but raises security exposure. Ship-tracking lists the vessel’s next destination as Poland, where long-term supply contracts exist between Poland’s Orlen SA and QatarEnergy — shipments under that deal were previously cancelled after a March drone strike on Qatar’s Ras Laffan. The move is notable as European gas prices trade near four-year highs; to avoid the Red Sea, many vessels now reroute via the Cape of Good Hope, adding nearly two weeks to single-leg voyages and lifting transport costs.

### 2026-10-19 16:06:36  #宏觀

【商務部：加強重點產品電商平臺聯網核查】10月19日，國新辦舉行介紹加快推進科技興商有關情況的新聞釋出會。商務部部長助理袁曉明介紹了近日釋出《關於加快推進科技興商的意見》（以下簡稱《意見》）的特點。他表示，本次《意見》是立足新發展階段，首次以“科技興商”為主題，出臺的一份綜合性、系統性檔案。核心是把科技創新作為貫穿商貿全鏈條的重要驅動力，推動科技與商貿深度融合。政策的主要特點，可以用三個字來概括：“新”“全”“實”。“新”，就是突出科技創新，適應消費新趨勢新變化。隨著人民生活水平提升，消費者更加註重產品的品質和消費體驗。根據商務部商務大資料顯示，今年1-9月，重點平臺智慧眼鏡銷售額增長1.1倍。人工智慧、物聯網、區塊鏈等新技術，在消費領域廣泛應用，科技創新日益成為推動消費擴容升級的重要舉措。《意見》緊扣新一輪科技革命和產業變革，既立足當前擴大消費的現實需要，著力啟用消費市場；又著眼長遠發展，以科技創新改造提升傳統商貿、培育壯大新興業態，為商貿高質量發展注入持久動力。

【機構：2026年光纖現貨報價倍數攀升，資料中心需求短缺或延續至2028年】10月19日，根據TrendForce集邦諮詢最新通訊產業研究，隨著AI資料中心、跨資料中心互連及高容量骨幹網路建設推升光纖需求，預估2026年全球光纖供需缺口將達1.8億芯公里，供給緊縮將帶動光纖成品現貨價格年增2.8至5倍以上。為此，電信廠商開始逐步調整採購模式，從短期轉向長約佈局，以提前鎖定光纖資源。該機構表示，由於AI資料中心周邊應用創造大幅需求，全球光纖供給自2025年底呈現緊張狀態，預計至少將持續至2028年。

### 2026-10-19 16:00:14  #國際

【我國在腦機介面領域實現國際標準新突破】記者今天瞭解到，國際標準化組織（ISO/IEC）釋出了我國牽頭制定的腦機介面參考架構國際標準：《資訊科技 腦機介面 參考架構》。該國際標準由我國專家擔任專案負責人，聯合義大利、韓國、英國、印度、美國、日本、瑞典、芬蘭、澳大利亞、德國等多個國家的專家共同完成制定，是我國在腦機介面國際標準化領域取得的又一項重要成果。隨著腦機介面逐步從科研探索走向醫療、健康消費、工業生產、交通安全等多領域落地應用，統一的基礎架構對於促進技術協同和產業規範發展日益重要。該標準圍繞“腦機介面是什麼、怎麼用、有什麼功能、如何實現”四個核心問題，系統梳理了典型實現模式，形成包括概念、應用、功能和實現的完整技術框架，解決了腦機介面不同技術路線和應用場景在系統組成、功能劃分和實現方式上存在差異的問題。（央視新聞）

### 2026-10-19 16:00:13  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Nickel Rebounds From Near 10-Month Low — Nickel traded around $15,400 per tonne, rebounding from its lowest level in nearly ten months as traders closed short positions following the recent decline. At the same time, easing US Treasury yields reduced concerns over tighter financial conditions, offering modest relief to commodity prices. However, high inventories, weak stainless steel demand and increased Indonesian ore supply continued to weigh on the outlook. Indonesia’s lower October nickel ore benchmarks and plans to increase production under supplementary mining quotas reinforced expectations of ample supply. The International Nickel Study Group also projected a 19,000-tonne global surplus in 2026, reversing its previous deficit forecast, with the surplus expected to widen to 70,000 tonnes in 2027. Reduced smelter purchasing further underscored weak underlying demand.

### 2026-10-19 15:54:45  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Stock Market #Importance 0

French Stocks Fall on Unrest Concerns — The CAC 40 fell 1.2% to 7,865 on Monday, reversing the previous week’s gains as mounting civil unrest and deepening political turmoil heightened concerns over France’s already strained public finances. The protests began last month in high schools in working-class northern suburbs of Paris, with students voicing concerns over the cost of living and dissatisfaction with the education system. Demands for increased public spending come as France faces mounting pressure to rein in its rapidly growing debt burden, complicating efforts to address social grievances without further straining public finances. The clashes also come at a critical juncture ahead of the presidential election in April. Financial stocks were among the biggest laggards, with BNP Paribas down 2.4%, Crédit Agricole falling 2.3% and Société Générale losing 2.3%. Notable decliners also included L’Oréal (-1%), LVMH (-1.7%) and Airbus (-1.8%).

FTSE 100 Falls on Monday — The FTSE 100 fell more than 0.8% on Monday, retreating after a rally of over 3% last week and tracking declines across European markets amid escalating protests in France, Belgium, Italy and Portugal. Banking stocks led the losses, with Barclays down 2.2%, HSBC falling 1.28%, and Lloyds Banking Group losing 0.9%. NatWest and Standard Chartered each declined 1.5%. Defence and aerospace shares also weakened, as Rolls-Royce dropped 1.6% and BAE Systems fell 2.6%. Mining stocks were lower, with Rio Tinto down more than 1% and Glencore shedding 0.8%. Antofagasta lost over 1.5% despite workers at its Chilean mine agreeing to a revised wage offer that would end a strike. In contrast, Shell and BP rose between 0.5% and 0.8%. Oil prices eased from a five-month high ahead of the European session, as traders weighed renewed Middle East fighting against signs that regional supply flows remain relatively resilient.

### 2026-10-19 15:33:33  #公司 #焦點 #國際

【商務部新聞發言人就盾構機等物項實施出口管制、最佳化調整挖泥船相關物項出口管制措施答記者問】問：2026年10月19日，商務部會同相關部門聯合釋出公告，對盾構機等物項實施出口管制、最佳化調整挖泥船相關物項出口管制措施。請問有何考慮？

答：根據《中華人民共和國出口管制法》等有關法律法規，10月19日，商務部會同相關部門釋出關於對盾構機等物項實施出口管制、最佳化調整挖泥船相關物項出口管制措施的公告，並於11月18日起正式實施。此前，我們已經向與中方建立了出口管制對話機制的有關國家和地區作了通報。

此次措施相關物項具有明顯的軍民兩用屬性，中國依法依規實施出口管制措施，符合國際通行做法，更好地維護了國家安全和利益，更好地履行了防擴散等國際義務。中國作為負責任大國，列管或最佳化調整相關管制措施，體現了堅定維護世界和平與地區穩定的一貫立場。中國政府願與各國一道維護全球產供鏈穩定暢通，中方將依法依規對相關出口許可申請進行審查，對符合規定的申請，中方將予以批准。同時，中方願透過雙邊出口管制對話交流機制，與相關方就出口管制政策和實踐開展溝通對話，共同積極促進、便利合規貿易。

### 2026-10-19 15:33:18  #焦點 #國際

【商務部新聞發言人就最佳化調整無人機相關物項出口管制措施答記者問】問：2026年10月19日，商務部會同相關部門聯合釋出公告，最佳化調整無人機相關物項出口管制措施。請問有何考慮？

答：根據《中華人民共和國出口管制法》等有關法律法規，10月19日，商務部會同相關部門釋出關於最佳化調整無人機相關物項出口管制措施的公告。相關措施將於11月18日起正式實施。此前，我們已經向與中方建立了出口管制對話機制的有關國家和地區作了通報。

此次措施涉及的無人機相關物項具有明顯的軍民兩用屬性，中方依法依規最佳化調整有關措施，目的是維護國家安全和利益，履行防擴散等國際義務。與此同時，中方致力於維護全球產業鏈供應鏈安全穩定，依法依規對相關出口許可申請進行審查，對符合規定的申請，中方將予以批准。

動態調整出口管制措施是國際通行做法。近年來，全球無人機產業和技術發展迅速，部分原來先進的產品如今已屬於大眾消費類產品。中方在實施出口管制措施過程中，積極聽取各方意見建議，並經認真評估後及時最佳化調整有關措施。本次對無人機出口管制措施的最佳化調整，就是基於業界反映的產業和技術最新情況，這充分反映中方堅決反對無人機被違規用於非和平目的，同時堅定支援中國企業在民用領域開展無人機國際貿易與合作。

中方願透過雙邊出口管制對話交流機制，與相關方就出口管制政策和實踐開展溝通對話，同時聽取各方有關意見建議，進一步認真履行防擴散等國際義務，積極促進、便利合規貿易。

French Borrowing Costs Rise as Unrest Deepens — France’s 10-year OAT yield climbed to 4.84%, approaching its highest level in more than two decades, as escalating social unrest and persistent political gridlock intensified pressure on the country’s already battered bond market. French government bonds have been among the hardest hit in a broader global bond selloff driven by renewed inflation concerns, expectations of higher interest rates, and growing worries over government debt levels.

### 2026-10-19 15:24:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Israel #GDP Growth Annualized #Importance 0

Israel Q2 GDP Growth Revised Back to 15.4% — Israel’s economy grew at an annualized rate of 15.4% in the second quarter of 2026, revised upward from 14.9% in the second estimate and returning to the level reported in the initial estimate. This followed a downwardly revised 1.5% contraction in the first quarter. Exports, excluding startups and diamonds, rose by 17.7%, up from 16.6% in the second estimate but below the initial estimate of 25.2%. Growth in government spending was revised upward to 22.4%, compared with 22.2% in the second estimate and 19.5% in the initial estimate. Meanwhile, private consumption growth was revised down to 14.6% from 15.0% in the previous estimate. Investment in fixed assets was also weaker than initially reported, with growth at 4.1% in both the second and latest estimates, compared with 6.3% in the initial estimate. For the first half of 2026, the annualized growth rate was 3.7%, up from 3.5% in the second estimate and 3.2% in the initial estimate.

India 10Y Yield Falls on US Treasury Decline — The yield on India’s 10-year G-Sec fell to around 7.36%, extending losses for another session as a sharp decline in US Treasury yields spurred value buying, offsetting inflation concerns from elevated crude oil prices. Indian yields tracked the US 10-year Treasury yield’s decline to around 5.05% amid expectations that the Federal Reserve may delay its next rate hike until December. Easing oil prices following a potential improvement in Russian supply also supported Indian yields. However, upside inflation risks persist, with September consumer inflation accelerating to 5.54% and potentially exceeding the RBI’s 6% upper tolerance limit in October. Meanwhile, expectations of further RBI tightening, growing bets on a 50-basis-point rate hike in December, and tighter banking-system liquidity continue to weigh on sentiment.

【Lu Liping: Policy shifting from short-term stimulus to long-term institutional reform】The National Bureau of Statistics released first‑three‑quarters data on the 19th. Lu Liping, professor at Renmin University School of Finance and deputy director of the China Banking Research Center, said the economy shows pronounced divergence across demand, prices and growth momentum and that policy is shifting from short‑term stimulus toward long‑term institutional reform. On demand, exports are strong while retail sales and fixed‑asset investment remain weak. On prices, producer‑side price moves have not fully transmitted to consumer prices. On growth momentum, old and new drivers display mixed strength.

### 2026-10-19 15:13:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Georgia #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Georgia Trade Deficit Widens to 9-Month High in September — Georgia’s trade deficit widened to USD 1,074.4 million in September 2026 from USD 862.5 million in the same month a year earlier, marking the largest deficit since December 2025. Exports increased 4.0% year-on-year to USD 731.5 million, while imports rose at a faster pace of 15.3% to USD 1,805.9 million. During the January–September period, exports surged 19.6% to USD 6,153.2 million, primarily driven by petroleum and petroleum oils (1,192.5%) and precious metal ores and concentrates (68.0%). Meanwhile, imports rose 5.3% to USD 14,382.0 million, supported by higher purchases of petroleum and petroleum oils (44.5%) and crude petroleum and petroleum oils, despite a decline in motor car imports (-13.3%). Imports recorded the largest increases from Russia (21.7%) and China (21.8%). Georgia’s trade deficit for the January–September period narrowed to USD 8,228.8 million from USD 8,513.2 million in the same period of 2025.

### 2026-10-19 15:12:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #Moldova #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Moldova Producer Prices Ticks Up in September — Producer prices in Moldova inched up to 0.3% year-on-year in September 2026 from 0.2% in the previous month. Price growth accelerated in manufacturing (1.3% vs 1.2% in August), lifted by significant gains in food products (1.4% vs 1.2%), tobacco (1.2% vs 0.7%), printing and reproduction of recorded media (2.0% vs 0.3%), and coke and refined petroleum products (4.0% vs 1.0%). Moreover, costs increased in mining and quarrying (9.2% vs 8.1%), while deflation remained steady in electricity, gas, steam, and air-conditioning supply (at -12.9%), as electric power generation (at -14.1%), electricity production (at -21.4%), and air-conditioning supply (at -10.7%) remained unchanged. On a monthly basis, producer prices recorded a flat reading in September, following a 0.1% increase in August.

【深圳市人社局: 倡導APEC會議期間實行靈活工作安排】近日，深圳市人力資源和社會保障局印發關於倡導APEC會議期間實行靈活工作安排的通知。2026年11月18日至19日，亞太經合組織（APEC）第三十三次領導人非正式會議將在深圳舉辦。承辦本次APEC會議，是深圳城市發展水平接受世界級檢閱、對標全球一流城市、提升城市能級的重大歷史機遇。為統籌做好外事高標準保障和城市正常執行，現倡導全市企業在APEC會議當週工作日期間（11月16日至20日）實行靈活工作安排。具體通知如下：（一）倡導企業安排職工居家遠端辦公，緩解通勤壓力，提高工作效率。企業要為職工居家遠端辦公提供便利條件，保障職工高效完成工作。 （二）倡導企業統籌安排職工帶薪年休假。企業應依法保障職工年休假權利，制定合理的年休假計劃，結合工作需要和職工個人意願，統籌安排職工帶薪年休假。 （三）對確需現場辦公或作業的崗位，企業應與職工充分協商，鼓勵職工錯峰上下班、彈性上下班，並優先採用公交、地鐵等方式綠色出行。

【韓國推出2萬億韓元貿易融資，扶持SK海力士供應鏈企業】韓國政府聯合SK海力士以及韓國國內四大商業銀行，推出規模達2萬億韓元（約合14億美元）的共生型貿易融資專案，面向半導體供應商。該專案旨在降低供應商融資成本，讓晶片行業的景氣紅利轉化為中小企業的擴產投資。韓國產業通商資源部表示，19日在京畿道龍仁市SK海力士半導體產業叢集舉辦共生貿易融資專案簽約儀式，產業部長金正官出席。韓國政府計劃將半導體領域共生貿易融資總規模擴充至至少5萬億韓元，持續加大供應商資金支援，使大型晶片廠商的行業紅利傳導至中小企業的投資與成長。本次簽約落地後，包含半導體在內的全行業共生貿易融資總規模已達4.4萬億韓元：汽車、造船、鋼鐵等板塊合計2.4萬億韓元，加上半導體板塊的2萬億韓元。韓國政府計劃持續加碼晶片產業扶持，同時把專案拓展至國防、核電、生物科技領域，力爭今年整體規模達到10萬億韓元。

【創業板算力ETF華夏（158041）探底回升，連續3天獲資金淨流入】10月19日，創業板算力ETF華夏（158041）探底回升，截至發稿漲0.12%，成交額3.88億元，流動性位居同類首位。資金流向方面，創業板算力ETF華夏（158041）已連續3日獲得淨申購，最新份額10.73億份，規模9.1億元。東方證券表示，隨著AI進入推理時代，Token工廠可以依託算力叢集與推理排程能力實現標準化Token供給，順應大模型推理需求持續增長趨勢，具備規模化降本潛力，看好其作為AI算力商業化核心載體的長期業務發展空間。國盛證券認為，美國科技企業今年已發行約2500億美元債券用於AI基建，預計全年發行規模將達3200億美元，同比增長約60%。同時，美債收益率持續攀升，10年期、30年期美債收益率均觸及二十多年來高位，大型科技企業集中發債導致市場資金緊張，AI資本開支正在與更廣泛的經濟體爭奪有限的資金供給。這意味著，算力叢集的擴張將面臨“錢從哪裡來、成本有多高”的問題，AI工廠的經濟學將成為產業下一階段的核心變數之一。繼續推薦算力產業鏈相關企業如光模組行業龍頭，建議關注國產算力產業鏈。創業板算力ETF華夏（158041）緊密跟蹤創業板算力基礎設施指數，指數聚焦AI算力基礎設施產業鏈，是一鍵佈局算力硬體核心環節的便捷工具。

TAIEX Ends at 4-Week Low as Tech Stocks Weigh — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, dropped 290 points, or 0.6%, to close at 46,822 on Monday, erasing gains from the previous session and touching its lowest level since September 17. The decline tracked losses on Wall Street on Friday amid a tech sell-off and concerns over AI-related investment. Most sectors traded in the red, including electronic technology, which dropped 0.5% after gaining 0.2% on Friday. TSMC, the world’s largest contract chipmaker, which accounts for more than 40% of the market’s total value, continued to decline, losing 1.0% following a 0.2% drop in the previous session. Meanwhile, MediaTek shed 1.3%, Hon Hai Precision lost 2.1%, and ASE Technology dropped 1.5%. The financial sector fell 0.9%, mainly weighed down by Fubon Financial Holding (-1.9%) and Cathay Financial Holdings (-2.2%). However, moderating oil prices, easing expectations of Fed rate hikes, and strong Chinese data, with Q3 GDP growth beating estimates, helped limit the decline.

### 2026-10-19 14:52:24  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Iron Ore Rises as Steel Output Rebounds — Iron ore futures climbed above CNY 680 per ton, recovering from four-year lows amid stronger crude steel production and better-than-expected economic growth in China. Official data showed that China’s daily crude steel output increased 1.9% month over month to 2.45 million tons in September, marking the first monthly rise since June, supported by robust exports and expectations of stronger seasonal domestic demand. Meanwhile, China’s economy grew 4.5% year over year in the third quarter, accelerating from 4.3% in the previous quarter and exceeding forecasts of 4.4%. However, underlying market conditions continued to weigh on iron ore, with ample global supplies, expanding mining capacity, and rising shipments expected in the fourth quarter. Sentiment was further dampened by the World Steel Association’s forecast of a 0.1% contraction in global steel demand in 2026, reversing its April projection of 0.3% growth.

Russia responded with strikes on Ukraine’s energy sector, including oil and gas facilities, carrying out at least 76 attacks over the past 24 hours. The escalation has cast fresh doubt on the near-term prospects for wheat shipments through the Black Sea, raising concerns about potential supply disruptions. Nanning Port, part of Beibu Gulf’s West River port, handled more than 668,000 tonnes in September, a single‑month record, as the port broadens beyond bulk minerals and grain into containerised operations including big‑bag potash unpacking, bauxite and iron‑ore decontainerisation for transshipment to bulkers, and high‑calcium stone container loading. Crude Oil Retreats — Crude Oil fell about 2% toward $99 per barrel on Monday following last week’s strong rally, as investors balanced persistent geopolitical tensions against signs that Middle Eastern oil supplies were recovering toward pre-conflict levels. ### 2026-10-19 14:11:03  #公司 #市場 #國際

【關稅仍不見蹤影 投資者對銅牛市心生警惕】自美國總統唐納德·特朗普重返白宮以來，市場擔心美國將對銅徵收關稅，引發全球庫存格局大變，銅價隨之飆升。但在特朗普第二個總統任期快要過去一半之際，不少多頭開始擔憂，如果關稅最終沒有落地，銅價可能大跌。直到幾周之前，紐約銅期貨價格都一直高於倫敦金屬交易所(LME)銅合約這個全球基準價格，讓貿易商能夠透過搶在關稅生效前將銅運往美國賺得前所未有的利潤。而銅持續流入美國，其他地區的庫存銳減，又推動LME銅價上月升至接近每噸15,000美元的紀錄高位。但隨著美國中期選舉臨近，各個市場的投資者都在思考，民主黨的強勁表現會否打亂特朗普的貿易議程，抑或反而促使他加倍實施關稅策略。對於銅多頭投資者而言，如果特朗普最終不願或無法推進銅關稅，後果可能十分嚴重。法國外貿銀行分析師警告稱，押注關稅的交易已在這個全球最重要工業大宗商品市場之一的市場中形成泡沫，如果關稅可能性被排除了，泡沫可能很快破裂。麥格理集團表示，在這種情況下，銅價可能跌向每噸10,000美元，但如果只是關稅宣佈得晚了一些，讓貿易商得以將甚至更多的銅運往美國，價格也可能漲至18,000美元。目前LME銅價在每噸14430美元附近。

NZX 50 Extends Losses, Ends at 4-Week Low — The NZX 50 shed 102 points, or 0.7%, to close at 13,652 on Monday, extending losses from the previous session and touching its lowest level since September 16. The index tracked a decline on Wall Street on Friday amid a continued tech sell-off. Traders were cautious ahead of the release of Q3 inflation data due this week, which could guide the RBNZ's interest rate decision. Fresh data also pressured sentiment, as New Zealand's trade deficit widened sharply in September, with imports rising more than exports. However, private sector figures for September capped the fall, as private sector growth hit a three-month high. Strong Chinese data and easing expectations of Fed rate hikes also helped limit the decline. The financials, communication services, and energy sectors mainly weighed on the index, with the biggest decliners being Infratil (-5.1%), Chorus (-3.1%), Colonial Motor (-2.7%), Mainfreight (-2.5%), and EBOS Group (-2.3%).

### 2026-10-19 13:40:51  #宏觀

【國務院學位辦釋出《關於做好專業學位研究生以實踐成果申請學位工作的意見》
】以實踐成果申請學位，是當前研究生教育改革發展的迫切需要。國務院學位委員會辦公室近日印發《關於做好專業學位研究生以實踐成果申請學位工作的意見》，在前期工程碩博士用實踐成果申請學位基礎上，推動5個專業學位類別試點先行。

今年畢業季，多所高校擴大了實踐成果申請專業學位的試點，部分碩博研究生不用撰寫傳統學位論文，而是憑實踐成果順利獲得學位。此次《意見》的出臺旨在為各地各校科學、規範開展以實踐成果申請學位工作提供政策遵循，確保學位授予質量。
《意見》強調，要堅持質量為先，完善以實踐成果申請學位的標準、機制和流程。國務院學位辦組織各專業學位研究生教育指導委員會，立足本專業學位類別人才培養規律，研製相應博士、碩士專業學位類別《基本要求》，明確不同型別（形態）實踐成果的來源與形式、內容結構、寫作規範及評價參考要素等。《基本要求》是學位授予單位制定實施細則的重要參考依據，學位授予單位要根據相關要求，結合本單位實際，分類完善各專業學位類別以實踐成果申請學位的實施細則，細化制定涵蓋實踐成果選題論證、過程指導、評價考核等關鍵環節的全流程工作指南，形成實踐成果可行性論證報告、成果鑑定書、專家評閱表等參考模板。

《意見》指出，要嚴格實踐成果貢獻認定標準，明確實踐成果須由研究生在導師指導下獨立完成或作為主要完成人承擔核心內容。同步完善學位評定委員會章程、博士碩士學位授予實施細則等管理制度，更新培養方案、校內外導師遴選、原創性成果及智慧財產權管理等配套檔案，構建全方位、全過程質量保障制度體系。

《意見》還強調要強化行業產業深度參與，明確行業導師的職責和作用，建立健全利益衝突迴避和公正評價機制。在培養方案制定、課程教學、實踐訓練、學位授予及成果轉化等方面，加強與行業產業的資源共享、標準共研和機制共建，促進人才培養、成果產出與產業需求緊密銜接。

據介紹，這項改革仍處在試行探索階段，要分批次推進各專業學位類別試行。下一步，國務院學位辦將組織指導各專業學位教指委研製本類別碩士、博士《基本要求》，首批擬在翻譯、農業、臨床醫學、會計、藝術5個專業學位類別先行先試。（央視新聞）

【美國華爾街利潤激增，推高紐約州稅收收入】紐約州稅收收入大幅增長，原因是交易市場交投火熱，華爾街利潤有望創下歷史新高。紐約州審計長托馬斯・迪納波利 16 日公佈，該州 2026-27 財年（2026 年 4 月至 2027 年 3 月）上半年稅收收入達 685 億美元（約合 10.83 萬億日元），較預期高出 73 億美元。迪納波利在宣告中指出：“州稅收增加，尤其是個人所得稅與企業稅收入增長，一部分源於金融市場走強。” 他同時表示：“但生活成本持續上漲，許多家庭依舊處境艱難。金融市場的強勁增長對一部分人而言是利好，必須讓經濟紅利惠及全體紐約州民眾。”迪納波利本月披露的資料顯示，華爾街今年上半年（1-6 月）利潤約 460 億美元，大幅超出預期，按當前增速推算，全年利潤有望突破 900 億美元，創下歷史新高。受此影響，獎金預計也將達到歷史峰值，進而拉動個人所得稅收入。在紐約市，證券行業從業人員超 20 萬人。在截至 6 月 30 日的財年，證券行業為紐約市貢獻約 80 億美元稅收，同比增長 15.8%；紐約全市稅收總額同比上漲約 7%。
